# Commentary v3 global finding review

You are the fresh global-scope decision agent for **1:7**. This is a
wider-reading task, not a prose-length or schema-compliance exercise. Inspect
the complete supplied global evidence independently. Do not read an existing
commentary, a previous decision ledger, another scope response, or repository
material outside this prompt.

Your question is:

**What becomes visible about this ayah only from the wider reading record?**

## Discovery posture

Err on the side of revelation. Surface every meaningful retrospective image,
long-range echo, reversal, pattern, counter-pattern, and surprise reading that
can return to the focus ayah through a named carrier. A reading does not need to
be canonical, conventional, majority-supported, or repeated by several readers.
Novelty, strangeness, outlier status, sole attestation, conflict with another
eligible reading, or difficulty of exposition is never by itself a rejection
reason.

Uncertainty changes `epistemic_status` and containment, not whether a real
discovery enters review. When the return path is genuine but the comparison is
fragile or analogical, preserve it as a clearly bounded exploratory finding.
Do not thin the finding set to make the eventual commentary easier.

Expansion still requires a traceable return path: name the focus carrier, the
later or wider trigger, the precise relation, and what becomes newly visible.
Do not turn analogy into etymology, recurrence into identity, or a wider image
into the focus word's literal sense.

## Global responsibility

Review every supplied regular and wide reader walk, retrospective surprise,
cross-run publication finding, assigned global HFT record, and per-ayah wider
connection row. The connection registry includes cross-surah targets and any
same-surah target beyond the declared pericope. Distinguish what the ayah can
yield alone from what later or wider context activates, sharpens, weakens, or
revises. A retrospective surprise
is especially valuable when its later trigger and its return path into this
ayah are explicit.

Use `primary_floor` as the supplied plain propositional `isolated_before`
footing. It is not a verdict: preserve what it plainly says while freely
recasting it into natural Turkish.

The connection rows carry prior attention labels such as `strong`, `medium`,
`weak`, or `no value`. Those labels are not verdicts and must not govern your
decision. Reassess every row. Exact target Arabic is supplied in
`target_evidence`, but target morphology and lexical analysis are not; do not
invent those missing details or a lexical identity. The source note is a lead,
not the limit of admissible discovery: a different bounded relation may be
recovered when it is freshly grounded in the supplied Arabic and returns
through a named focus-ayah carrier.

Some connection rows have `origin: "derived_reciprocal_seed"`. At least one
target-side source row meaningfully linked back toward the focus ayah, so its
`source_note` and `source_direction_label` are discovery evidence from that
direction—not a focus-side verdict and not proof that the relation works
identically in reverse. Reassess the return path from this focus ayah using the
exact supplied target Arabic. Preserve a real reverse discovery with an
appropriate boundary; reject it only when a specific edge fails.

Rows with `origin: "derived_reciprocal_counterevidence"` expose target-side
`no value` or `reject` rows even when the focus mapping has no authored edge.
They are preserved source-side negative evidence: inspect whether they reveal
a true asymmetry, a failed return path, or a relation the source-side review
missed.
They are not a veto and do not excuse a fresh focus-side pass. Any connection,
including a derived seed whose `origin` is determined by the presence of a
nomination, may carry both kinds of `reciprocal_evidence`. Inspect every nested
row, including same-surah evidence beyond the pericope. Keep disagreements
visible; never collapse the two directions into one judgment. The authored row,
when present, and every nested row have distinct `connection_evidence_ref`
values. Judge each one separately before deriving the connection-level
disposition.

Every authored connection and every nested reciprocal row carries
`source_row_role`; a derived connection exposes its roles through its nested
`reciprocal_evidence` rows.
`ranked_review` identifies the first 100 reviewed candidates;
`missing_ayah_suggestion` identifies the source agent's later high-recall
follow-up additions. Both invite fresh analysis. A suggestion is not
pre-accepted, and neither role nor its label may veto a grounded discovery.

When an authored row or reciprocal item has `source_target_is_range: true`, its
note was authored for the complete range. Exact Arabic for every component is
supplied in `source_target_range_evidence`. Test the exposed component on its
own wording, keep sequence-level material at range scope, and never attribute
another component's feature or morphology to it.

The supplied candidates are a review docket, not an accepted list and not a
quota. Review every candidate and the underlying wider evidence. You may recover
a missed finding when that evidence supports a materially distinct change in
the ayah's reading.

The packet's `hft_evidence.assigned_records` contain HFT nominations with a
wider-than-pericope anchor and individually split HFT reader-synthesis items;
each is a mandatory-review candidate with no presumptive outcome. Its exact raw
payload and exact available anchor Arabic are supplied by its sole `support_id`.
Review every one. Source binding, packet scope, confidence, or a missing
independent branch registry may limit certainty, but may never decide the
outcome. Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only; HFT-stated segmentation,
word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless
independently supplied. Treat unanchored synthesis as an inventory item to
trace, decompose, represent, refer, or reject—not as an acceptable surah thesis.
If you accept, narrow, refer, or represent an HFT item as an exact duplicate,
carry its support ID and preserve its source containment, live alternatives,
analogy limits, and any “not a lexical gloss” boundary. A duplicate target must
preserve the evidence union.

Do not search only for confirmation of supplied candidates. Independently scan
every wider-reading record for uncandidate discoveries and record every
meaningful contact opportunity. For every cited branch, explain the distinctive
facet that matters; a branch ID or generic gloss alone is false coverage.

Use the complete focus-branch atlas, not only candidate-cited branches. Silently
scan every atlas branch and every one of its supplied facets against the wider
records and supplied connections. A multi-facet branch is not reviewed by
choosing one representative facet. `review_facets` is the packet's normalized
authoritative facet list, including stable IDs for older source images that
lacked numbered concept-map facets. Record `atlas_facets_tested[]` for every
branch with a plausible wider contact or an existing citation in a supplied
candidate, an emitted contact, or an accepted finding, listing all tested facet
IDs and a branch-level `result` (`nominated`,
`no_independent_trigger`, or `scope_referral`), and any contact refs. The facet
statements stay in the packet and need not be copied into the response. Emit
findings only for real contacts; do not enumerate branch-by-branch combinations.
Every tested facet row is a `{facet_id, result}` object whose `result` is only
`contact`, `no_independent_trigger`, or `scope_referral`; a `nominated` branch
must land in an accepted finding or a linked
accepted/narrowed/represented/referred contact.

Every accepted finding must re-enter through a word, relation, or act already
present in the focus ayah. Reader agreement is not authority; an outlier is not
valuable merely because it is surprising. Provenance qualifications govern
`epistemic_status` and containment, not visibility. They do not override a
grounded return path, and they are not independent evidence that a reading is
false.

Reject only when you can name the failed edge: no ayah-local return path, no
wider trigger stated in the supplied record or freshly identifiable in the
exact supplied Arabic, no stated or freshly grounded relation, no changed
reading or payoff, an uncontained thesis, or an exact duplicate. Missing target
morphology is not by itself a rejection reason when the finding stays within
the exact supplied Arabic and a relation grounded by either the supplied note
or fresh focus-side assessment; do not invent lexical or morphological details.
Different or countervailing wider discoveries remain eligible.
For a duplicate, name the accepted target, explicitly show that local anchor,
mechanism, direction, and reader payoff are all the same, and preserve the
union of its evidence. Merely saying that it is already represented is
insufficient. Canonical status, source count, confidence labels, vividness, or
anticipated prose length never decides.
Scope overreach justifies rejection only when no bounded global core survives;
otherwise narrow the claim or issue a `scope_referral`.

Work in two internal phases. First answer the bold global question and discover
real carrier/trigger/relation/payoff returns without drafting coverage rows.
Then serialize those completed judgments. Coverage rows verify a judgment; they
must not generate one. Before returning JSON, re-ask the bold question and
recover any wider visibility that connection or facet accounting nearly
displaced.

Boundary example: valid coverage says that carrier `X`, facet `F002` (“a
boundary channels movement”), and a distant blocked destination together make
an otherwise generic motion read as constrained passage, with a stated reader
payoff. False coverage merely lists `root_x/B_y`, `F002`, or “boundary/path”
without the wider trigger, return path, and changed reading.

Do not write a surah thesis and do not write the commentary yet. Acceptance
must be settled before prose facility can influence it.

## Required response

Return one JSON object with:

All model-created stable refs in this response (`finding_ref`, `proposal_key`,
`contact_ref`, and `referral_ref`) must begin with `global:` so their identities
remain unambiguous when the three independent ledgers are reconciled.

- `schema_version: "commentary-v3-scope-review-v2"`;
- `identity` containing `ayah_ref: "1:7"`, `lane: "global"`,
  `lane_packet_sha256: "912acbb26c7f7d491b268cc00d37c4a79b643eb2a3138bac67da9c7ad9fabc60"`, and
  `authoring_request_sha256: "51308ffe948979bf4464964921e89d9d2ac142483679400442f4dd1ed08328a5"`;
- `ayah_ref: "1:7"` and `lane: "global"`;
- `coverage_complete`, true only after every supplied candidate, every assigned
  HFT record, and every supplied wider-reading record is reviewed;
- `support_coverage[]`, one row per supplied non-HFT wider-reading support
  record, with
  its exact `support_id`, `result` (`accepted`, `narrowed`, `represented`, or
  `rejected`), related `finding_refs`, and concise `reason`;
- `connection_coverage[]`, one row per supplied connection, with
  `connection_ref`, `target_ref`, `result` (`accepted`, `narrowed`,
  `represented`, or `rejected`), related `finding_refs`, and a specific reason
  independent of the prior label. Include `evidence_row_results[]`, exactly one
  row per supplied authored or reciprocal `connection_evidence_ref`, each with
  its own result from the same enum, related `finding_refs`, and a specific
  reason. The connection-level `finding_refs` are the exact union of the nested
  rows; its result is the first present result in this precedence: `accepted`,
  `narrowed`, `represented`, `rejected`;
- `atlas_facets_tested[]` as defined above, so a cited or plausible branch cannot
  hide behind a generic gloss or one representative facet;
- `contact_opportunities[]`, one row per meaningful wider contact, with stable
  `contact_ref`, `focus_carrier`, `wider_trigger`, `relation`,
  `isolated_before`, `wider_after`, `reader_payoff`, `containment`, complete
  `support_ids`, complete `branch_refs`, complete `connection_refs`,
  `disposition` (`accept`, `narrow`, `represented`, or `scope_referral`), the
  accepted `finding_refs` for an accepted, narrowed, or exactly represented
  contact, and any `cross_root_boundary` needed to prevent analogy from being
  mistaken for lexical identity. A failed attempted edge stays in candidate,
  connection, or facet coverage with its specific failure; it is not a contact
  and receives no `contact_ref`;
- `candidate_decisions[]`, one row per supplied candidate, with `candidate_id`,
  `decision` (`accept`, `narrow`, `reject`, or `scope_referral`), concise
  `reason`, `failed_edge` when rejected, related `accepted_finding_refs`, and
  nullable `duplicate_of`, complete `excluded_branch_refs`, and
  `branch_exclusion_reasons[]` (`branch_ref`, `reason`). Use empty arrays when no
  branch was dropped. Do not repeat the candidate's original evidence arrays;
  they remain available by `candidate_id` in the packet;
- `scope_referrals[]`, one row for every candidate decision, atlas row, or
  contact that uses `scope_referral`, with stable `referral_ref`, `origin_ref`,
  `referred_lane`, `focus_carrier`, `mechanism`, `reader_payoff`, `containment`,
  complete `support_ids`, `branch_refs`, `connection_refs`, and `contact_refs`,
  plus a specific `reason`. A referral is lossless work for another lane, not a
  rejection shorthand;
- `new_findings[]`, a compact map for uncandidate discoveries, each with a
  stable `proposal_key`, its `accepted_finding_ref`, a nonempty exact
  `contact_refs` list naming its originating contacts, and any originating
  `connection_refs`; put the finding content and evidence only in
  `accepted_findings`;
- `accepted_findings[]`, one row for every accepted existing or new finding,
  with `finding_ref`, complete `candidate_ids`, complete `proposal_keys`,
  `title`, `isolated_before`, `wider_after`, `wider_trigger`, `claim`,
  `mechanism`, `reader_payoff`, `containment`, complete `support_ids`,
  `branch_refs`, `connection_refs`, `contact_refs`, and `branch_contributions`,
  and `epistemic_status`. Supply one or more distinct contributions for every
  cited `branch_ref`; give separate rows when one branch activates multiple
  distinctive facets or independently anchored contributions, rather than
  flattening them. Each row states the exact single `facet_id` from that
  branch's `review_facets`, distinctive facet, carrier, independent anchor,
  actual contribution, and boundary. The same facet ID must have result
  `contact` in that branch's tested-facet ledger;
- `friction_notes[]` for live ambiguity, evidence gaps, source qualification,
  scope referrals, or decisions a reconciler should inspect.

Every supplied candidate must receive exactly one decision. Every accepted or
narrowed candidate must appear in `accepted_findings`. Referred and rejected
material remains visible in the decision ledger. Do not rank accepted findings.
Every assigned HFT record is already one supplied candidate; qualified metadata
cannot erase it.
An accepted finding cites only the support, branches, and connections its final
claim actually uses. When narrowing drops an original branch, record it in the
owning candidate decision's `excluded_branch_refs` and
`branch_exclusion_reasons`; do not preserve discarded branches as false semantic
coverage. Every `scope_referral` disposition must have one stable payload in
`scope_referrals`.
Semantic completeness matters more than cosmetic JSON regularity; never omit a
reading merely to simplify the response envelope.

After returning the decision ledger, remain in this same conversation. A later
follow-up will supply the reconciled, locked global set for prose preparation.

فَلْيَكْتُبْ وَلْيُمْلِلِ ٱلَّذِى عَلَيْهِ ٱلْحَقُّ وَلْيَتَّقِ ٱللَّهَ رَبَّهُۥ وَلَا يَبْخَسْ مِنْهُ شَيْـًۭٔا ۚ فَإِن كَانَ ٱلَّذِى عَلَيْهِ ٱلْحَقُّ سَفِيهًا أَوْ ضَعِيفًا أَوْ لَا يَسْتَطِيعُ أَن يُمِلَّ هُوَ فَلْيُمْلِلْ وَلِيُّهُۥ بِٱلْعَدْلِ ۚ وَٱسْتَشْهِدُوا۟ شَهِيدَيْنِ مِن رِّجَالِكُمْ ۖ فَإِن لَّمْ يَكُونَا رَجُلَيْنِ فَرَجُلٌۭ وَٱمْرَأَتَانِ مِمَّن تَرْضَوْنَ مِنَ ٱلشُّهَدَآءِ أَن تَضِلَّ إِحْدَىٰهُمَا فَتُذَكِّرَ إِحْدَىٰهُمَا ٱلْأُخْرَىٰ ۚ وَلَا يَأْبَ ٱلشُّهَدَآءُ إِذَا مَا دُعُوا۟ ۚ وَلَا تَسْـَٔمُوٓا۟ أَن تَكْتُبُوهُ صَغِيرًا أَوْ كَبِيرًا إِلَىٰٓ أَجَلِهِۦ ۚ ذَٰلِكُمْ وَأَشْهِدُوٓا۟ إِذَا تَبَايَعْتُمْ ۚ وَلَا يُضَآرَّ كَاتِبٌۭ وَلَا شَهِيدٌۭ ۚ وَإِن تَفْعَلُوا۟ فَإِنَّهُۥ فُسُوقٌۢ بِكُمْ ۗ وَٱتَّقُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ ۖ وَيُعَلِّمُكُمُ ٱللَّهُ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ بِكُلِّ شَىْءٍ above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"source_coverage":{"assigned_hft_record_count":10,"authored_connection_count":203,"authored_connections_with_reciprocal_evidence":74,"connection_count":279,"connection_evidence_row_count":353,"connection_scopes":{"inter_surah":203,"inter_surah_reciprocal_evidence":76},"derived_reciprocal_counterevidence_count":26,"derived_reciprocal_seed_count":50,"docket":{"accounted_seed_count":126,"by_disposition":{"docket_mandatory":74,"docket_optional":34,"parse_failed":6,"quarantined":12},"by_source_type":{"channel":31,"cross_run_publication":9,"hft":18,"qac_morpheme":5,"v12_reader_walks":13,"v12_reader_walks_wide":6,"word_analysis":44},"discovered_seed_count":126,"docket_candidate_count":108,"mandatory_candidate_count":74,"optional_candidate_count":34,"selection_eligible_candidate_count":83,"selection_ineligible_candidate_count":25},"hft_anchor_evidence_count":0,"inter_ayah_source":{"directional_review_row_count":209,"directional_source_available_for_pointer_dereference":true,"directional_source_directory":"quran-data/data/analysis/inter-ayah","incoming_cross_surah_origin_count":150,"incoming_origin_count":156,"incoming_same_surah_origin_count":6,"integrity_warnings":[],"manifest_available":true,"manifest_document_record":{"directional_review_record_count":209,"mirrored_record_count":156,"record_count":365,"sha256":"478ed5fea0664680851f55710ab62f956ed7e7c60523f5c03c5c9ce4355e1340"},"manifest_sha256":"4a64e9696428027e61968b118d532143b801bbe25a31ed643427d481502160a7","operational_fallback_used":false,"parent_directional_source_is_not_concatenated":true,"reciprocal_counterevidence_row_count":37,"reciprocal_nomination_row_count":119,"self_reiteration_row_count":0,"self_reiterations_are_not_reciprocal_evidence":true,"source_directory":"quran-data/data/analysis/inter-ayah/reciprocal","source_document":"quran-data/data/analysis/inter-ayah/reciprocal/focus_1_7_cutoff_100.tsv","source_document_sha256":"478ed5fea0664680851f55710ab62f956ed7e7c60523f5c03c5c9ce4355e1340","source_id":"quran-data/inter-ayah-row-reciprocal-v2"},"lane_branch_count":42,"lane_candidate_count":38,"lane_support_count":38,"missing_hft_anchor_refs":[],"quran_text_source":{"ayah_count":6348,"source_id":"quran-data/quran-uthmani","source_path":"quran-data/data/text/quran-uthmani.tsv","source_sha256":"9c89cdb0be69ced88ce38bae183b336ae6a6e4eed2f1ae3db0f7e811b8e729dd"},"source_types":["cross_run_publication","hft","v12_reader_walks","v12_reader_walks_wide"],"total_hft_record_count":28,"unresolved_branch_refs":[]},"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B001","root_001525/B002","root_001525/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_06055c87212ed0d1b967","text":"2. **Favor as softening and carrying the journey further.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `ن ع م` B002 is softness and ease, and B010 is adding or carrying an action further; fixed `ص ر ط` B001 supplies the journey, while 1:5 `ع ب د` B005 supplied the image of a smoothed, passable road. **Structural cue:** service, the request for a straight road, and `أنعمت` occur consecutively. **Role and abductive move:** the softness branch makes the course traversable and the increase branch supplies continued propulsion; I infer that favor both eases resistance and advances recipients beyond an initial start. **Reading change:** `أنعمت عليهم` is not a static grant of comfort but an enabling action that keeps travelers moving on a worked, passable course.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001119/B003","root_001119/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0f36fbc62679e503ebfd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:7:5\",\"root_001119\",[\"B003\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"“Değil” yapısı, yalnız iki grubu adlandırmaz; başarısız yönlerin istenen yolun yerine geçmesini engelleyen etkin bir sınır çizer.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001092/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_12bb8312c0542bd204d1","text":"5. **Anger as hardened material obstruction.** Evidence: `غ ض ب B004` hard compact rock, `غ ض ب B008` tough hide/folded leather like a shield, activated by `ص ر ط B003` cutting sword passing through and `ق و م B008` straightness. Mechanism: anger is not only affect; its branches materialize as hard compactness that blocks or armors against the path. The packet supplies hard rock/tough hide, cutting-through, and straightness; I infer obstruction. Abductive move: a straight path must cut through or avoid hardened states that retaliation produces. Reading change: “المغضوب عليهم” carries a material image of hardened blockage, not only a moral category.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001092/B004","root_001092/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-7","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_199c318205594bb038c5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:7:6\",\"root_001092\",[\"B004\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Öfke yolu, yumuşayıp ilerlemek yerine katılaşarak doğru geçişe direnç gösteren bir engel niteliği kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B014","root_000913/B005","root_001525/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1d101a10ff4fa5bf13cb","text":"4. **Herding: favored creatures are led; lost creatures become strays.** Evidence: `ن ع م B005` grazing livestock, especially camels; `ض ل ل B005` stray animal in a wasteland; `ه د ي B008` supported/swaying walking and heavy camels; `ر ب ب B014` herd or gathered wild cattle. Mechanism: the animal-material branches convert the social contrast into a pastoral mechanism: favor gathers and leads, while ضلال leaves an animal in a place of loss with no known owner. The packet supplies livestock, straying, supported walking, and herd gathering; I infer the shepherding relation. Abductive move: “upon them” can be read as care resting on a group so that they do not become ownerless strays. Reading change: the final ayah becomes a request to be gathered and led under care, not scattered into wasteland-loss.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001119/B001","root_001119/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_24660a1f6c1b672da5fd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:7:5\",\"root_001119\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Bu dışlama, bozulmuş bir güzergâhın yerine doğru yolu geçirerek yönü onaran bir yeniden yönlendirme olarak da okunur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_26e74cbe074ec7f62d23","text":"1. **The road is identified by marked recipients of favor.** Evidence: `ص ر ط B001` road/path, `ن ع م B001` bestowed favor and pleasant well-being, `ن ع م B003` praise as excellent, and `ع ل م B002` distinguishing mark/landmark from 1:2. Mechanism: the path is not defined abstractly but through “those” upon whom favor has landed; they function as markers that make the road recognizable. The packet supplies path, favor, praise, and sign/landmark branches; I infer that favored people become road-markers. Abductive move: if a path is hard to find, the visible trace of those already favored can identify it. Reading change: “صراط الذين أنعمت عليهم” reads as a marked social route, not merely a class of fortunate people.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B003","root_000913/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_365654424b4fa972f333","text":"5. **Straying as loss of locatability and retained orientation.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `ض ل ل` B003 is losing or failing to locate something, B004 is loss of retention, and B005 is the stray animal left without a known owner. Earlier `ر ب ب` B001 and `م ل ك` B002–B003 supplied owner/master and dominion, while `ع ل م` B002 and `ه د ي` B001 supplied landmarks and guidance. **Structural cue:** `الضالين` comes after a road identified by recipients and after an ownership-guidance sequence. **Role and abductive move:** the owner image supplies affiliation, landmarks supply remembered orientation, and loss removes both practical locatability and retention; I infer that departure from the marked road makes the owner relation ineffective from the traveler’s side even though sovereignty remains. **Reading change:** straying is not only choosing a wrong proposition but becoming unfindable within the course and losing the marks needed to return.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B001","root_001040/B002","root_001525/B001","root_001583/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_444c19c6ea742f6f3ff6","text":"1. **The path becomes socially embodied.** Lexical evidence: ص ر ط B001, straight road; ن ع م B001, bestowed favor; ع ل م B002 in 1:2, distinguishing mark; ه د ي B001 in 1:6, guidance. Structural cue: 1:6 asks for the path; 1:7 restates it as the path of people, not an abstract route. Assigned role: the favored ones are living markers of the road. Abductive move: the packet supplies road, favor, and sign-marking; I infer that people become waymarks by received favor. Reading change: the path reads as a transmitted social trajectory rather than a private mental direction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001119/B003","root_001119/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_47a1511077438e3fe419","text":"3. **“Not” as active differentiation, not a footnote.** Lexical evidence: غ ي ر B005, otherness/difference/exception/negation; غ ي ر B003, altering/substituting one thing for another. Structural cue: غير stands between the favored path and two failure groups. Assigned role: غير performs the boundary operation that keeps the requested path from being confused with nearby alternatives. Abductive move: the packet supplies difference and substitution; I infer that negation actively changes orientation by excluding false routes. Reading change: the final clause reads as path-engineering through differentiation, not merely as a warning.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B003","root_000913/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-8","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4ec8819150111fc272e9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:7:9\",\"root_000913\",[\"B003\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sapma, yalnız yanlış görüşe saplanmak değil, yolu bulduracak işaretleri akılda tutamayıp güzergâh içinde bulunabilirliğini yitirmektir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001119/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_503a0f836461d855fb42","text":"2. **Negation splits the path away from retaliation and aimless deviation.** Evidence: `غ ي ر B005` otherness/exception/negation, `غ ض ب B001` intensified anger toward retaliation, and `ض ل ل B001` going astray from guidance/aim. Mechanism: “غير … ولا” creates two exclusions: the path is not the retaliation-field and not the deviation-field. The packet supplies negation, anger, and straying; I infer that the syntax functions as route-separation. Abductive move: guidance must name avoided trajectories as well as desired direction, because error can resemble movement. Reading change: the ayah becomes a boundary-drawing map: favor is read together with explicit separation from anger and loss.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B002","root_001092/B001","root_001119/B002","root_001525/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:6","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6accdb3b8ad3c99b7c7e","text":"6. **The final contrast as legal-moral settlement.** Lexical evidence: د ي ن B002/B003 in 1:4, recompense and debt; غ ي ر B002, legal compensation alternative; غ ض ب B001, anger toward retaliation; ن ع م B001, benefaction. Structural cue: “Day of dīn” precedes “favored / angered / astray.” Assigned role: the final triad shows the account’s possible settlements: favor, wrath, or loss. Abductive move: the packet supplies recompense/debt/compensation and the triadic outcome; I infer a legal-moral sorting mechanism. Reading change: 1:7 reads as the requested verdict-shape of the worshipper’s account, not only a route description.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B005","root_001525/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_72331d64272c252d52b9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:7:3\",\"root_001525\",[\"B005\"]],[\"1:7:9\",\"root_000913\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İyilik insanları koruma altında toplayıp götürür; sapma ise aidiyetin ve izlenebilirliğin yitirildiği bir kayboluşa dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001119/B003","root_001583/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_72666b8d6a66c0599f09","text":"3. **Repair and substitution away from damaged routes.** Evidence: `غ ي ر B003` altering/substituting one thing for another, `غ ي ر B001` repair through provision/watering/setting right, activated by `ر ب ب B002` repair/nurture in 1:2 and `ه د ي B001` guidance in 1:6. Mechanism: غير does more than exclude; it also activates replacement and repair, as if the requested path substitutes a rightly provisioned route for one ending in anger or loss. The packet supplies repair, substitution, nurture, and guidance; I infer a corrective route-change. Abductive move: to be guided is to have one route changed out for another, not only to receive a label saying “wrong.” Reading change: “غير المغضوب” reads as active redirection and repair, not bare negation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B001","root_001092/B001","root_001119/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-9","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_77cc67a41212a7004d31","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:7:5\",\"root_001119\",[\"B005\"]],[\"1:7:6\",\"root_001092\",[\"B001\"]],[\"1:7:9\",\"root_000913\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Son dışlamalar, kimi insanların merhametin dışında olduğunu söylemekten çok, merhametli yol göstermenin insanı hangi sonuçlardan koruduğunu belirler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B001","root_001092/B001","root_001092/B003","root_001092/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_786881ab3a1895ad9f69","text":"4. **Wrath as hardened opposition.** Lexical evidence: غ ض ب B001, intensified anger toward retaliation; غ ض ب B003, defiance/opposition; غ ض ب B004, hard compact rock; ر ح م B001 in 1:1–1:3, tenderness. Structural cue: “angered” is opposed to mercy/favor and placed before “astray.” Assigned role: غضب is a hardened, oppositional state that attracts judgment. Abductive move: the packet supplies anger, defiance, hardness, and earlier tenderness; I infer that wrath marks a hardening against the merciful order. Reading change: المغضوب عليهم reads as people fixed in resistant hardness, not only recipients of an emotion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9190f3f9d9c06dab799b","text":"Looking back from the end, the basmalah’s mercy now covers even the exclusions: the final negations are not outside mercy but define what merciful guidance saves from. 1:5 also changed the reading of this ayah: the group on the favored path are not passive beneficiaries only; their way is entered through worship, help-seeking, and the smoothing of the path. The most surprising activation was pastoral: `ن ع م B005` and `ض ل ل B005` make favor versus loss feel like gathered herd versus stray animal, which gives “اهدنا” a strongly protective and communal force.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001092/B003","root_001092/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_94013367ea7e1e1035c1","text":"4. **Wrath as the consequence of opposition hardening into resistance.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `غ ض ب` B003 is defiance or opposition, B001 is intensified displeasure rising toward retaliation, and B004 supplies the material image of hard compact rock. Earlier `د ي ن` B001 and `ع ب د` B003 supplied obedience and submissive service, while fixed `ن ع م` B002 supplies the counterimage of softness. **Structural cue:** the wrath category is set against favored recipients within a path contrast. **Role and abductive move:** opposition supplies conduct, wrath supplies consequence, and compactness supplies resistant condition; I explicitly infer the arrows from defiance to wrath and from sustained opposition to hardening. **Reading change:** exploratorily, `المغضوب عليهم` becomes a processual and material counterpath—resistance that closes into hardness—not an arbitrary emotional label.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001092/B001","root_001092/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a4ab0f58cbb96268580f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:7:6\",\"root_001092\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Öfkeye uğrama, keyfî bir etiket değil, ısrarlı karşı koymanın misillemeye yönelen öfkeyi doğurduğu süreçsel bir sonuç olarak görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b83299e71138ad9cb7e5","text":"Because this is the final ayah, no later ayah supplied a further surprise. Its own backward activation was strong: it reinterpreted earlier mercy as protective kinship, رب as care/ownership, دِين as account-settlement, worship as path-smoothing, and guidance as rescue from disappearance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B001","root_001525/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c3ebdb7782066f5271b5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:7:1\",\"root_000858\",[\"B001\"]],[\"1:7:3\",\"root_001525\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Doğru yol, soyut bir çizgi değil, iyilik görmüş insanların hayatında izlenebilir hale gelen toplumsal ve tarihsel bir güzergâhtır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001119/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d735a193883d983c1f0b","text":"3. **`غير` as a boundary operation, not another group label.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `غ ي ر` B005 is otherness, difference, exception, and negation; B003 is substitution or displacement of one thing by another. **Structural cue:** `غير ... ولا ...` divides the favored path from two failed relations, after 1:5 twice fronted one exclusive addressee and 1:6 requested one straight road. **Role and abductive move:** the fixed word acts as a gate that excludes contrary outcomes and blocks their substitution for the requested course; I infer that exclusive orientation requires this categorical noninterchangeability. **Reading change:** the close of the surah specifies guidance partly by an active boundary: what must not be allowed to occupy the place of the favored road.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001273/B009","root_001525/B001","root_001525/B002","root_001583/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d8b1c35dc4fb51550f56","text":"2. **Favor as softening and making the route livable.** Lexical evidence: ن ع م B001, favor/good condition; ن ع م B002, softness/easeful living; ه د ي B004, gracious gift; ق و م B009 in 1:6, subsistence/support. Structural cue: the favored group is opposed to anger and loss. Assigned role: favor makes the path inhabitable, supported, and softened. Abductive move: the packet supplies softness/support and the negative alternatives; I infer that favor alters the material feel of the route. Reading change: أنعمت عليهم reads not only as “gave them blessings” but as “made their way livable and sustained.”","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B001","root_000913/B002","root_000913/B003","root_000913/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_daf0dfa39305b1c6ccbb","text":"5. **Going astray as disappearance, loss, and ownerless wandering.** Lexical evidence: ض ل ل B001, deviation from guidance/aim; ض ل ل B002, hiddenness/disappearance; ض ل ل B003, losing/failing to locate; ض ل ل B005, stray animal in wasteland; ر ب ب B001/B002 in 1:2, owner/caretaker. Structural cue: the surah began with Lord/caretaker and now ends with those who are lost or stray. Assigned role: ضلال is the breakdown of guidance, visibility, ownership, and care. Abductive move: the packet supplies Lord/caretaker and lost/stray branches; I infer that straying is not only error but loss from a care-system. Reading change: الضالين reads as the ownerless/lost condition from which the prayer seeks rescue.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_000858/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_de7e08bc50e11f63232f","text":"1. **A road constituted by bestowed human precedent.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `ص ر ط` B001 is the road, and fixed `ن ع م` B001 is pleasant well-being and benefaction delivered to another. **Structural cue:** the repeated `صراط` is appositionally specified by people who received an action, not by additional spatial coordinates; 1:2 `ر ب ب` B002 and 1:5 `ع و ن` B001 have already supplied nurture and aid. **Role and abductive move:** favor makes the people path-bearers and their history makes the route socially visible; I infer that benefaction installs and maintains them on it. **Reading change:** the straight road is an inherited, embodied course recognizable in recipients, not an abstract line discovered alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_debd2d6cbd8c0aa676c3","text":"No later ayah exists after 1:7 in the clipped packet window, so the retrospective pass produced no later-ayah activation or correction for this fixed ayah.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B002","root_001525/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f0afb759aeaf28f9c892","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:7:3\",\"root_001525\",[\"B002\",\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İyilik, yolculara yalnız rahatlık veren durağan bir nimet değil, yolun direncini azaltıp onları yürüyüşte ileri taşıyan etkin bir destek olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
